独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 37621553234 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

轻微指引缺失上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F3 · 迁移示例与 Repository 章节未提醒 createdAt/updatedAt 需写入方自带

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/db · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

冻结源码证实框架不自动写 createdAt/updatedAt：Repository 策略仅把 defaultValue、db.generated、autoIncrement/increments、optimisticLock 视为自动取值来源（E1），关系写入把 nullable===false 且无 defaultValue 的列列入 requiredOnCreate（E2）。nocobase-db SKILL 迁移示例写 collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false }) 且无默认值（E3），Repository 用法与易错清单也未提时间戳（E4）。应用照示例建表（E27）并在 service.ts 统一 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 于每次写入自带时间戳（E24）。属 guidance-gap，改进空间由源码与指引直接证实。

  • 职责与公开约定：框架承诺的自动取值来源仅 defaultValue/db.generated/autoIncrement/optimisticLock，无 createdAt/updatedAt 注入分支（E1）；无默认值的 NOT NULL 列须写入方显式提供（E2）。SKILL 示例却声明 NOT NULL createdAt 且无默认，并未提示写入需自带（E3、E4）。 E1 E2 E3 E4
  • 实际行为证据：实现层面没有任何自动时间戳补全；应用因此必须每次写入附带时间戳，注释与 helper 明确说明 NOT NULL 无数据库默认（E24），迁移也以 notNull 无默认声明（E27）。 E1 E24 E27
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用严格照 SKILL 示例建表（E27），随后在 service.ts 用 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 绕行框架不自动写时间戳的行为（E24），对应指引缺口，而非应用误用 API。 E24 E27
  • 环境因素：NOT NULL 失败源于数据库列无默认值与写入未提供值，可由 schema（E27）与策略判定（E2）解释；非外部服务、凭据或安装环境造成，故环境解释不适用。 E2 E27
  • 工厂因素：迁移示例与 Repository 章节均由工厂预置 nocobase-db SKILL 提供，缺口在指引本身，未见工厂脚本/模板覆盖造成；缺失材料处保留未知。 E3 E4
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开能力未提供自动时间戳入口：策略只认列出的取值来源（E1），Repository 章节也未给出时间戳 helper 或约定（E4），故照指引实现只能自行补齐。 E1 E2 E4

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

@nocobase/db 的 Repository 策略只把 defaultValue/db.generated/autoIncrement/optimisticLock 视为可自动取值来源，不存在 createdAt/updatedAt 自动注入；nocobase-db SKILL 的迁移示例却是 collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false }) 且无默认值，Repository 章节也未提。结果：按示例建表后，任何未自带时间戳的写入都会命中 NOT NULL。

实际影响
开发者照示例迁移后，常规 createOne/updateOne 会因缺少 createdAt/updatedAt 失败，需要自行发现并统一封装时间戳（应用用 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 绕行）。
建议改法
在 nocobase-db SKILL 迁移示例与 Repository 写入章节明确：框架不自动写时间戳；NOT NULL 无默认的时间列必须由每次写入提供，并给出统一的 stamp helper 示例。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
按 nocobase-db SKILL 迁移示例声明 NOT NULL 且无默认值的 createdAt/updatedAt，然后用 Repository.createOne/updateOne 写入
应有能力 / 行为
指引说明框架不会自动写时间戳，并提示写入方需自带 createdAt/updatedAt
实际观察
示例与实际实现均无自动时间戳，且 SKILL 未提示；应用需自行发现并封装 createStamp/updateStamp
应用绕行与代价
应用在每次写入统一附带 createdAt/updatedAt（service.ts:86-106）
改进后的验收标准
迁移/Repository 章节明确该约定并给出 stamp 示例；照指引实现的写入不再触发 NOT NULL 失败
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 6 条
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:263–274E1

Repository 策略只把 defaultValue、db.generated、autoIncrement/increments、optimisticLock 识别为可自动取值来源；没有任何 createdAt/updatedAt 自动注入分支。

        const hasValueSource = field.defaultValue !== undefined ||
            field.db?.generated !== undefined ||
            field.autoIncrement === true ||
            field.type === 'increments' ||
            collection.optimisticLock?.field === name;
        if (hasValueSource)
            continue;
        invalid('INVALID_POLICY', `create.scope reads Field "${name}", which this create can never set: it is absent from create.fields and create.defaults, and the Collection gives it no default.`, {
            collection: collection.name,
            field: name,
            path: ['policy', 'create', 'scope'],
        });
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:183–194E2

关系写入把 nullable === false 且 defaultValue === undefined 的字段列入 requiredOnCreate，说明无默认值的 NOT NULL 列必须由写入方显式提供。

                cardinality: toOne ? 'one' : 'many',
                targetCollection: relation.target,
                ...(throughCollection
                    ? {
                        through: {
                            collection: throughCollection.name,
                            writableFields: throughFields.map((field) => field.name),
                            requiredOnCreate: throughFields
                                .filter((field) => field.nullable === false &&
                                field.defaultValue === undefined)
                                .map((field) => field.name),
                        },

迁移示例 collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false }) 未给默认值，也没有任何「写入时需自带时间戳」的提示。

  name: '202609020001_create_orders',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('orders', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('orderNo', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('status', { length: 32, nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.unique('orderNo');
      collection.index('status');

app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:236–248E4

Repository 用法与「易错边界」清单详列 filter/values/select、writePolicy 等，但未说明 createdAt/updatedAt 不会自动生成。

Resolve the manager once — in a route factory, or as a constructor dependency of a service — not inside every handler. `db.repository()`, `db.query()`, `db.builder()`, `db.collections()` and `db.transaction()` take the connection name as their last argument and use the default connection when it is omitted, so single-connection code never names one.

`filter`, `values`, `select` and `sort` each accept a builder callback or the equivalent serializable JSON AST, and mean the same thing either way. They do not accept a shorthand string array such as `['id', 'name']` or `['-createdAt']`. Field names are Collection logical names, never column names. A write returns a result object — the record is `result.record`, alongside `result.version` and the created relation targets — rather than the record itself. `findMany` is awaited for an array or iterated asynchronously to consume a large result incrementally.

The methods are `findOne`, `findMany`, `count`, `exists`, `aggregate`, `groupBy`, `createOne`, `createMany`, `updateOne`, `updateMany`, `upsertOne`, `deleteOne` and `deleteMany`; `describeMutation` and `validateMutation` report capabilities and pre-validate input, and `withPolicy` returns a scoped Repository.

Boundaries that are easy to get wrong:

- `findOne` takes the first match; `updateOne` and `deleteOne` require exactly one; `upsertOne` requires a unique equality condition.
- `updateMany` and `deleteMany` refuse an empty scope. Pass `all: true` only when the task explicitly calls for every row — never to make an error go away.
- `createMany` and `updateMany` do not accept nested relation writes. `createOne` accepts `create` and `connect` on a relation, not the full set.
- `disconnect` detaches; `delete` removes the target record. A relation target update or delete stays inside the current parent record's scope.
- Repository does not apply the application's permissions. A route handler decides what the caller may see and change _before_ anything reaches `filter`, `values` or `select`. `writePolicy` defaults to `true` for an internal Repository and `false` for one reached from an API route, so a route-facing Repository needs an explicit allow list.
app/server/hr/service.ts:86–106E24

应用注释说明托管集合 NOT NULL createdAt/updatedAt 无数据库默认，因此 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 在每次写入自带时间戳——这是对框架无自动时间戳的绕行。

/**
 * The managed collections declare NOT NULL `createdAt`/`updatedAt` with no
 * database default, exactly as the framework's own tables do, so every write
 * supplies its own timestamps.
 */
function createStamp(): { createdAt: Date; updatedAt: Date } {
  const now = new Date();
  return { createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
}

function updateStamp(): { updatedAt: Date } {
  return { updatedAt: new Date() };
}

/**
 * The notification table is an append-only log with no `updatedAt` column, so
 * it stamps only the creation time.
 */
function createdStamp(): { createdAt: Date } {
  return { createdAt: new Date() };
}
app/database/main/migrations/202611010001_create_hr_departments.ts:24–25E27

应用迁移 collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull()/updatedAt 无默认值，与 db SKILL 示例一致。

      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][指引缺失] 迁移示例与 Repository 章节未提醒 createdAt/updatedAt 需写入方自带

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.17）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

冻结源码证实框架不自动写 createdAt/updatedAt：Repository 策略仅把 defaultValue、db.generated、autoIncrement/increments、optimisticLock 视为自动取值来源（E1），关系写入把 nullable===false 且无 defaultValue 的列列入 requiredOnCreate（E2）。nocobase-db SKILL 迁移示例写 collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false }) 且无默认值（E3），Repository 用法与易错清单也未提时间戳（E4）。应用照示例建表（E27）并在 service.ts 统一 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 于每次写入自带时间戳（E24）。属 guidance-gap，改进空间由源码与指引直接证实。

- 职责与公开约定：框架承诺的自动取值来源仅 defaultValue/db.generated/autoIncrement/optimisticLock，无 createdAt/updatedAt 注入分支（E1）；无默认值的 NOT NULL 列须写入方显式提供（E2）。SKILL 示例却声明 NOT NULL createdAt 且无默认，并未提示写入需自带（E3、E4）。；证据：E1（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:263–274）、E2（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:183–194）、E3（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–99）、E4（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:236–248）
- 实际行为证据：实现层面没有任何自动时间戳补全；应用因此必须每次写入附带时间戳，注释与 helper 明确说明 NOT NULL 无数据库默认（E24），迁移也以 notNull 无默认声明（E27）。；证据：E1（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:263–274）、E24（app/server/hr/service.ts:86–106）、E27（app/database/main/migrations/202611010001_create_hr_departments.ts:24–25）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用严格照 SKILL 示例建表（E27），随后在 service.ts 用 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 绕行框架不自动写时间戳的行为（E24），对应指引缺口，而非应用误用 API。；证据：E24（app/server/hr/service.ts:86–106）、E27（app/database/main/migrations/202611010001_create_hr_departments.ts:24–25）
- 环境因素：NOT NULL 失败源于数据库列无默认值与写入未提供值，可由 schema（E27）与策略判定（E2）解释；非外部服务、凭据或安装环境造成，故环境解释不适用。；证据：E2（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:183–194）、E27（app/database/main/migrations/202611010001_create_hr_departments.ts:24–25）
- 工厂因素：迁移示例与 Repository 章节均由工厂预置 nocobase-db SKILL 提供，缺口在指引本身，未见工厂脚本/模板覆盖造成；缺失材料处保留未知。；证据：E3（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–99）、E4（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:236–248）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开能力未提供自动时间戳入口：策略只认列出的取值来源（E1），Repository 章节也未给出时间戳 helper 或约定（E4），故照指引实现只能自行补齐。；证据：E1（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:263–274）、E2（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:183–194）、E4（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:236–248）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

@nocobase/db 的 Repository 策略只把 defaultValue/db.generated/autoIncrement/optimisticLock 视为可自动取值来源，不存在 createdAt/updatedAt 自动注入；nocobase-db SKILL 的迁移示例却是 collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false }) 且无默认值，Repository 章节也未提。结果：按示例建表后，任何未自带时间戳的写入都会命中 NOT NULL。

**影响**：开发者照示例迁移后，常规 createOne/updateOne 会因缺少 createdAt/updatedAt 失败，需要自行发现并统一封装时间戳（应用用 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 绕行）。

**建议改动**：在 nocobase-db SKILL 迁移示例与 Repository 写入章节明确：框架不自动写时间戳；NOT NULL 无默认的时间列必须由每次写入提供，并给出统一的 stamp helper 示例。

**验收标准**：迁移/Repository 章节明确该约定并给出 stamp 示例；照指引实现的写入不再触发 NOT NULL 失败

F4 · 用户名 3–30 长度约束只在初始管理员段出现，create 方法表未复述

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

authentication SKILL 只在 initialAdmin 段声明『用户名 3–30 个字母/数字/下划线/点』（E15），user-lifecycle 的 create({ name, email, username?, password }) 方法表仅写 email 与 username 归一化为小写（E16）；冻结的 create() 实现只校验密码、解析可选用户名并调用 internalAdapter.createUser，未复述或前置长度校验（E13）。repair 记录显示 2 字符演示用户名 `hr` 触发 Better Auth『Username is too short』，应用改名为 hrlead/hradmin 绕行（E55）。属 guidance-gap，在 create/update 附近复述约束有明确改进空间。

  • 职责与公开约定：SKILL 初始管理员段承诺用户名 3–30 规则（E15），但 user-lifecycle 的 create 方法表只承诺小写归一化（E16），公开方法面未承诺该长度约束；实现同样未在 create 内校验（E13）。 E15 E16 E13
  • 实际行为证据：create() 实际只校验密码并委托 internalAdapter.createUser（E13）；输入 2 字符用户名时运行时报『Username is too short』（E55），说明长度约束由底层 Better Auth username 插件而非文档化方法裁决。 E13 E55
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用以演示账号用户名 `hr`（2 字符）调用账户创建而失败，随后改名绕行（E55），符合指引未在 create 处复述约束导致的误用，而非应用逻辑错误。 E55 E16
  • 环境因素：依赖的 Better Auth username 插件默认最小长度解释了运行时拒绝（E55），但这不解释文档缺口；文档缺口由 SKILL 与 user-lifecycle 文本本身独立成立（E15、E16）。 E55 E15 E16
  • 工厂因素：相关 SKILL 与 reference 由工厂预置，create 方法表位于工厂指引内，缺口在指引本身；未见工厂注入/模板覆盖导致，缺失材料处保留未知。 E15 E16
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：已存在能力：工厂文档在 initialAdmin 段已记载 3–30 规则（E15），只是未复述到 user-lifecycle 的 create/update 处（E16），故属可在现有文档内补全的指引改进而非缺失能力。 E15 E16

authentication SKILL 的初始管理员段写明用户名 3–30 个字母/数字/下划线/点，但 user-lifecycle 的方法表 create({ name, email, username?, password }) 只写「归一化为小写」，未复述长度约束；Better Auth 的 username 插件会以「Username is too short」拒绝 2 字符用户名。

packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:91–113E13

UserAdministrationService.create 校验密码/可选用户名并断言身份唯一，创建用户与凭据账户。

    async create(input) {
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        validatePassword(input.password, context.password.config);
        const username = optionalUsername(input.username);
        const email = normalizedEmail(input.email);
        await this.assertIdentityAvailable({ email, username });
        const user = await context.internalAdapter
            .createUser({
            name: requiredText(input.name, 'User name'),
            username,
            email,
            emailVerified: false,
            disabledAt: null,
        }, { method: 'admin' })
            .catch(throwIdentityConflict);
        await context.internalAdapter.createAccount({
            accountId: user.id,
            providerId: 'credential',
            userId: user.id,
            password=[REDACTED] context.password.hash(input.password),
        });
        return (await this.get(user.id));
    }
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:142–156E14

disable() 置 disabledAt 并删除全部会话、断开 realtime；enable() 清除 disabledAt。停用后旧会话不可再用。

    async disable(userId) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.updateUser(userId, {
            disabledAt: new Date(),
        });
        await this.revokeSessions(userId);
        return (await this.get(userId));
    }
    async enable(userId) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.updateUser(userId, { disabledAt: null });
        return (await this.get(userId));
    }
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:138–140E15

初始管理员段声明「Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots」，但该约束只出现在 initialAdmin 段落。

## Initial administrator

Before the first seed run, set `users.initialAdmin.username`, `users.initialAdmin.email` and `users.initialAdmin.password` in application configuration to customize the bootstrap account. Omitting the entire node preserves `nocobase` / `admin@nocobase.com` / `admin123`; an explicit node requires a nonempty password and defaults the username to `nocobase` and the email to `admin@nocobase.com`. Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots, emails must be valid addresses, and both are stored lowercase. Initialization only runs when the user table is empty, hashes the password and grants the newly created account root permission through the authorization plugin. After initialization, configuration changes do not reset accounts or rerun the recorded seeds. The root permission seed selects the administrator by the same configured username. Use the user administration service for subsequent account changes. Do not edit historical seeds or add a competing administrator seed.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/user-lifecycle-and-deployment.md:20–32E16

create({ name, email, username?, password }) 的方法表只写「email 与 username 归一化为小写」，未复述 3–30 长度约束，容易让调用方误用 2 字符用户名。

| Method                                              | Effect                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `list({ page, pageSize, search, status, userIds })` | Paged users; `pageSize` caps at 100; `status` is `'enabled'` or `'disabled'`                           |
| `get(userId)`                                       | One user or `undefined`                                                                                |
| `create({ name, email, username?, password })`      | Creates the user and a credential account; email and username are normalized to lower case             |
| `update(userId, { name?, email?, username? })`      | `username: null` clears it                                                                             |
| `disable(userId)`                                   | Sets `disabledAt`, deletes every session, disconnects realtime; sign-in returns `403 ACCOUNT_DISABLED` |
| `enable(userId)`                                    | Clears `disabledAt`; the user signs in again, old sessions stay gone                                   |
| `resetPassword(userId, password)`                   | Rehashes and revokes sessions                                                                          |
| `revokeSessions(userId)`                            | Deletes sessions and disconnects realtime without changing state                                       |
| `withConnection(connection)`                        | Binds every operation to a caller-owned transaction                                                    |

authentication SKILL 的初始管理员段写明用户名 3–30 个字母/数字/下划线/点，但 user-lifecycle 的方法表 create({ name, email, username?, password }) 只写「归一化为小写」，未复述长度约束；Better Auth 的 username 插件会以「Username is too short」拒绝 2 字符用户名。

轻微易用性改进上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F2 · composite 下 require() 恒返回 conditional：指引应把「用 can()/authorize() 而非 require()」放到显著位置

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

冻结源码证实公开授权语义：can() 仅对 permit 返回 true，require() 对任何非 permit 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError→403（E6）；composite 仅当所有底层 check 都 permit 才 permit，否则返回 conditional（E7），故以 require() 门禁 composite 必然被拒。指引确已在 Enforcement rules 写明『require 需要 unconditional permit 并拒绝 conditional decisions，业务端点用 authorize』（E57），runtime-api 末句亦写明（E12）；但 Implement 步骤 4（E10 所引 SKILL 第 60 行）只讲 authorizeRepository/authorize()，core-api 的 require 行只说『use for unconditional capabilities』而未点名 composite（E58），因此『把 can()/authorize() 而非 require() 放到步骤/require 行显著处』仍有真实改进空间。这是 usability-improvement，不要求运行时失败；合理替代解释（指引已覆盖）只覆盖部分章节，未覆盖步骤 4 与 require 行。

  • 职责与公开约定：公开语义：can 仅 permit 为真；require 非 permit 即抛错（E6）；composite 非全 permit 即 conditional（E7）。SKILL Enforcement rules 已声明 require 拒绝 conditional、业务端点用 authorize（E57），runtime-api 末句同样（E12）；core-api require 行仅称 use for unconditional capabilities，未把 composite→conditional→require 拒绝串明（E58）。 E6 E7 E57 E12 E58
  • 实际行为证据：冻结实现确认 require 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError；repair 日志显示复合门禁端点 departments/employees/leave-requests 403，而未经 require 的 dashboard/notifications 200（E50）；应用最终在 routes/hr.ts 以 context.can() 门禁并注释说明原因（E25）。 E6 E7 E50 E25
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用最初以 require 门禁 composite 资源而整组 403，随后改为 context.can() 做动作级门禁（E25、E50）。这是按指引入口选择错误并被绕行，非框架缺陷，故支持指引显著性改进。 E25 E50
  • 环境因素：403 来自应用/框架授权决策而非外部服务：同一进程与凭据下 dashboard 200（E50），排除服务、凭据或部署环境解释。 E50 E6
  • 工厂因素：相关章节由工厂预置 skill 提供（SKILL/runtime-api/core-api），未发现工厂注入、QA/构建脚本或模板覆盖造成该语义缺失；指引本身即待改进对象。缺失工厂材料处保留未知。 E12 E57 E58
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：已有公开入口与文档确实陈述了 require 拒绝 conditional（Enforcement rules、runtime-api 末句），但步骤 4 与 core-api 的 require 行没有可直接照做的 can()/authorize() 组合示例，故存在能力但推荐路径仍可改进。 E57 E12 E58

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

authorization.js 中复合决策只有在所有底层 check 都 permit 时才返回 permit，否则返回 conditional（带 conditions）；require() 对非 permit 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError → 403。runtime-api.md 第 137 行末句虽写了「require rejects conditional decisions; can does not enforce rows」，但 SKILL 步骤 4 与 core-api 的 require 行未把这一组合讲清。

实际影响
按 SKILL 步骤 4 用 require() 门禁复合资源时，所有复合端点会一起返回 403（本次 departments/employees/leave-requests 全 403，dashboard/notifications 因未走 require 而 200），排查成本高。
建议改法
在授权 SKILL 步骤 4 与 core-api 的 require 行显著说明：复合决策可能是 conditional，require() 会拒绝；动作级门禁应使用 can()，需要数据策略时使用 authorize() 并绑定 conditions.database。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
按授权 SKILL 步骤 4 / 公开 API，用 context.require() 对 composite 资源做动作级门禁
应有能力 / 行为
指引在推荐用法处明确指出 composite 决策是 conditional、require() 必拒绝，并给出 can() 或 authorize() 的正确示例
实际观察
runtime-api.md 仅在第 137 行末句提示该语义，SKILL 步骤 4 与 core-api require 行未提示；应用按 require() 实现后整组复合端点返回 403 PERMISSION_DENIED
应用绕行与代价
应用改用 context.can() 做动作级门禁（routes/hr.ts:102），行级与字段范围由 service 消费 conditions.database 执行
改进后的验收标准
指引在 composite/require 相关章节显著说明该拒绝行为并给出 can()/authorize 示例；照指引实现的门禁端点不再整组 403
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 6 条
packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:93–124E6

can() 仅当 effect 为 permit 时返回 true；require() 对非 permit 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError（应用回 403 PERMISSION_DENIED）；authorize() 返回完整 decision。

            identity,
            authorize: authorize,
            can: async (input) => (await this.authorizeWithGrants(request(input), grants, constraints, false)).effect === 'permit',
            require: async (input) => {
                const decision = await authorize(input);
                if (decision.effect !== 'permit') {
                    throw new AuthorizationDeniedError(decision);
                }
            },
            snapshot: () => this.snapshot(identity, grants, constraints),
        };
    }
    async authorizeWithGrants(request, grants, constraints, resolveConditions = true) {
        const handler = resourceTypeHandler(this.resourceTypes, request.resource.type);
        if (!handler) {
            return deny('UNKNOWN_RESOURCE_TYPE', `No resource type "${request.resource.type}" is registered`);
        }
        const stored = request;
        try {
            const unrestricted = (await grants.unrestricted?.({
                principal: request.principal,
                ...(request.subjects === undefined
                    ? {}
                    : { subjects: request.subjects }),
            })) === true;
            const decision = unrestricted
                ? await handler.authorizeUnrestricted(stored)
                : await handler.authorize(stored, { grants, constraints });
            if (decision.effect === 'conditional' &&
                decision.conditions === undefined) {
                return deny('INVALID_CONDITIONAL_DECISION', `Resource type "${handler.type}" returned a conditional decision without conditions`);
            }
packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:174–186E7

复合授权：所有底层 check 都 permit 才返回 permit，否则返回 conditional（带 conditions），因此 require() 对 composite 一定拒绝。

        };
        for (const plugin of this.plugins)
            Object.assign(conditions, plugin.composeConditions?.(checks));
        // A denied underlying check becomes a policy that reaches no records.
        return {
            effect: checks.every((check) => check.decision.effect === 'permit')
                ? 'permit'
                : 'conditional',
            conditions,
            reasons: [
                ...decision.reasons,
                ...checks.flatMap((check) => check.decision.reasons),
            ],
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:68–71E10

SKILL 说明 composite/database.collection 是目录类型，复合动作只组合自己声明的 grant；简单 CRUD 用 authorizeRepository，多 scope/流程用自定义 handler。

- `settings`, `composite` and `database.collection` are catalog types: a check on an unregistered item or action is denied with `RESOURCE_ACTION_NOT_SUPPORTED`. `page`, `user`, `notification` and Hub types are record types that declare actions only; their grants use `id: '*'` for every record.
- A composite action composes exactly the grants it lists, never another composite. A data scope targets the one collection its grant actions name, whose type must declare `recordAccess` (`database.collection` does). Grant page access separately; a page grant never authorizes a server endpoint.
- `can` is for feature visibility. `require` needs an unconditional permit and rejects conditional decisions. Business endpoints use `authorize`, reject denial or missing policies, and consume `conditions.database` without resolving collection grants again.
- For simple business CRUD, keep the Repository route definition and bind each method to its composite action with `authz.database.authorizeRepository`. Multi-scope actions and workflow transitions need custom handlers. `authz.database.policyFor` folds collection grants; without its third argument it does not respect a business-action boundary.
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:135–137E12

runtime-api 在复合决策段末句写明「require rejects conditional decisions; can does not enforce rows」——这是指引中唯一直接点出该组合的句子。

```

A composite decision's `conditions` are `{ type: 'composite', checks, database }`: one `RepositoryPolicy` per composed collection, built from that action's grants only, and `checks` with the underlying decisions. A denied collection check yields `false` for that collection rather than denying the whole feature, and a Repository refuses to run under it; a granted check whose data scope selects nothing, such as a user in no department, yields a scope that matches no rows, so reads return an empty result without a special case. For `submit`, use both `conditions.database.quotes` and `.projects` inside the same transaction, check the quote's actual parent project through the project policy and include the expected state in the update predicate. Do not replace these policies with `authz.database.policyFor` without an operation: another action's grants could then widen access. `require` rejects conditional decisions; `can` does not enforce rows.
app/server/routes/hr.ts:92–108E25

应用的路由门禁最终用 context.can({ resource: HR_RESOURCE, action })；注释解释 require 拒绝 conditional，不能门禁 composite。

  const require = async (
    context: AuthorizationContext,
    action: HrAction,
  ): Promise<void> => {
    // A composite action composes database grants, whose own decisions are
    // `conditional` because they carry a row scope. `require` rejects anything
    // other than a plain `permit`, so it cannot gate a composite; `can` is the
    // action-level check. Row scoping and field confidentiality are enforced by
    // the service, which is where a data scope that resolves to nothing turns
    // into an empty result rather than a refusal.
    if (await context.can({ resource: HR_RESOURCE, action })) return;
    throw new HrError(
      'PERMISSION_DENIED',
      'AUTHORIZATION_DENIED',
      `Missing permission: ${HR_RESOURCE.id}.${action}`,
    );
  };

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][易用性改进] composite 下 require() 恒返回 conditional：指引应把「用 can()/authorize() 而非 require()」放到显著位置

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/authorization`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.11）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

冻结源码证实公开授权语义：can() 仅对 permit 返回 true，require() 对任何非 permit 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError→403（E6）；composite 仅当所有底层 check 都 permit 才 permit，否则返回 conditional（E7），故以 require() 门禁 composite 必然被拒。指引确已在 Enforcement rules 写明『require 需要 unconditional permit 并拒绝 conditional decisions，业务端点用 authorize』（E57），runtime-api 末句亦写明（E12）；但 Implement 步骤 4（E10 所引 SKILL 第 60 行）只讲 authorizeRepository/authorize()，core-api 的 require 行只说『use for unconditional capabilities』而未点名 composite（E58），因此『把 can()/authorize() 而非 require() 放到步骤/require 行显著处』仍有真实改进空间。这是 usability-improvement，不要求运行时失败；合理替代解释（指引已覆盖）只覆盖部分章节，未覆盖步骤 4 与 require 行。

- 职责与公开约定：公开语义：can 仅 permit 为真；require 非 permit 即抛错（E6）；composite 非全 permit 即 conditional（E7）。SKILL Enforcement rules 已声明 require 拒绝 conditional、业务端点用 authorize（E57），runtime-api 末句同样（E12）；core-api require 行仅称 use for unconditional capabilities，未把 composite→conditional→require 拒绝串明（E58）。；证据：E6（packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:93–124）、E7（packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:174–186）、E57（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:70–70）、E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:135–137）、E58（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/core-api.md:13–13）
- 实际行为证据：冻结实现确认 require 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError；repair 日志显示复合门禁端点 departments/employees/leave-requests 403，而未经 require 的 dashboard/notifications 200（E50）；应用最终在 routes/hr.ts 以 context.can() 门禁并注释说明原因（E25）。；证据：E6（packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:93–124）、E7（packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:174–186）、E50（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0041.txt:2594–2600）、E25（app/server/routes/hr.ts:92–108）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用最初以 require 门禁 composite 资源而整组 403，随后改为 context.can() 做动作级门禁（E25、E50）。这是按指引入口选择错误并被绕行，非框架缺陷，故支持指引显著性改进。；证据：E25（app/server/routes/hr.ts:92–108）、E50（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0041.txt:2594–2600）
- 环境因素：403 来自应用/框架授权决策而非外部服务：同一进程与凭据下 dashboard 200（E50），排除服务、凭据或部署环境解释。；证据：E50（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0041.txt:2594–2600）、E6（packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:93–124）
- 工厂因素：相关章节由工厂预置 skill 提供（SKILL/runtime-api/core-api），未发现工厂注入、QA/构建脚本或模板覆盖造成该语义缺失；指引本身即待改进对象。缺失工厂材料处保留未知。；证据：E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:135–137）、E57（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:70–70）、E58（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/core-api.md:13–13）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：已有公开入口与文档确实陈述了 require 拒绝 conditional（Enforcement rules、runtime-api 末句），但步骤 4 与 core-api 的 require 行没有可直接照做的 can()/authorize() 组合示例，故存在能力但推荐路径仍可改进。；证据：E57（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:70–70）、E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:135–137）、E58（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/core-api.md:13–13）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

authorization.js 中复合决策只有在所有底层 check 都 permit 时才返回 permit，否则返回 conditional（带 conditions）；require() 对非 permit 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError → 403。runtime-api.md 第 137 行末句虽写了「require rejects conditional decisions; can does not enforce rows」，但 SKILL 步骤 4 与 core-api 的 require 行未把这一组合讲清。

**影响**：按 SKILL 步骤 4 用 require() 门禁复合资源时，所有复合端点会一起返回 403（本次 departments/employees/leave-requests 全 403，dashboard/notifications 因未走 require 而 200），排查成本高。

**建议改动**：在授权 SKILL 步骤 4 与 core-api 的 require 行显著说明：复合决策可能是 conditional，require() 会拒绝；动作级门禁应使用 can()，需要数据策略时使用 authorize() 并绑定 conditions.database。

**验收标准**：指引在 composite/require 相关章节显著说明该拒绝行为并给出 can()/authorize 示例；照指引实现的门禁端点不再整组 403

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:93–124` — can() 仅当 effect 为 permit 时返回 true；require() 对非 permit 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError（应用回 403 PERMISSION_DENIED）；authorize() 返回完整 decision。
- `packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:174–186` — 复合授权：所有底层 check 都 permit 才返回 permit，否则返回 conditional（带 conditions），因此 require() 对 composite 一定拒绝。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:68–71` — SKILL 说明 composite/database.collection 是目录类型，复合动作只组合自己声明的 grant；简单 CRUD 用 authorizeRepository，多 scope/流程用自定义 handler。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:135–137` — runtime-api 在复合决策段末句写明「require rejects conditional decisions; can does not enforce rows」——这是指引中唯一直接点出该组合的句子。
- `app/server/routes/hr.ts:92–108` — 应用的路由门禁最终用 context.can({ resource: HR_RESOURCE, action })；注释解释 require 拒绝 conditional，不能门禁 composite。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-2.jsonl/part-0041.txt:2594–2600` — repair-2 关键证据：hrlead 登录 200、/api/hr/dashboard 200，但 /api/hr/departments、employees、leave-requests 全部 403 PERMISSION_DENIED（reason AUTHORIZATION_DENIED，message permission-sets:hr-hr），而 /api/hr/notifications 200。整组复合门禁端点被拒，正是 require() 拒绝 conditional 决策的表现。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 37621553234 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。

Four defects blocked the endpoints: (1) gating a composite action with `authz.require()` always returned 403 because a composite composes database grants whose decisions are `conditional`, and `require` accepts only `permit` — switched the route guard to `authz.can()`, which is the action-level check, leaving row scoping and field confidentiality to the service; (2) every `createOne`/`updateOne` failed on `created_at NOT NULL` because NocoBase 3 does not auto-fill timestamps — stamped `createdAt`/`updatedAt` explicitly (the notifications table has no `updatedAt`); (3) the Better Auth username plugin rejects usernames shorter than 3 characters, so the `hr` demo account could never be created — renamed it to `hrlead`; (4) employee numbers generated from a row count collide after an offboard, and seeded leave requests carried a null `userId` because the in-memory employee map predated account creation — derive the next number from the highest existing one, keep the map in step, and fall back to the employee record for ownership and notification. Also placed the `hr.portal` composite in the permission workspace to clear the startup warning. Verified end-to-end against a real dev server: sign-in for all four demo roles, dashboard counts, department/employee/leave lists with correct field confidentiality, 401 unauthenticated, 403 for the employee's employee-list and decide attempts, rejection-reason enforcement, rejection-and-resubmit flow with notifications, and onboarding/offboarding with account enable/disable. `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm test` (250) and `pnpm build` pass.",

以 80 人 HR 系统为测试场景，评审 NocoBase3 的数据库/迁移、授权、认证与用户生命周期、服务端路由、通知投递五项基础能力。框架基础能力总体可用：最终轮 verify-3 的 C01 通过。两处主要摩擦都集中在指引/使用体验而非实现缺陷：(1) 复合资源下 require() 恒拒绝 conditional 决策，导致整组端点 403，指引只在 runtime-api 末句点到，应用随后改用 can() 修复（F2）；(2) @nocobase/db 不自动写 createdAt/updatedAt，但迁移示例与 Repository 章节未提醒，写入方必须自带时间戳（F3）。另有用户名 3–30 约束仅在初始管理员段出现的小缺口（F4）。通知插件具备持久化站内信能力，应用选择自建集合属正常组合（未扣分）。UI 未经本评审人工查看：当前模型无法解析截图，ui=not-reviewed。

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 1

做得好的地方
F1 · 复合资源 + 数据库权限的组合能表达 HR 的动作、字段与数据范围边界

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/authorization · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization

⌄

授权模型把面向用户的操作声明为复合资源（hr.portal），每个动作只组合自己声明的数据库 grant；字段可读/可写集合与数据 scope 均由 defineDatabasePermission 声明，应用再在 service 消费 conditions.database 做行级与字段级过滤。

提供的帮助
框架的基础授权原语足以承载“身份证件仅本人/人事可见、主管只见部门员工姓名岗位与请假”这类业务边界，且决策与实现可审计。
应保留的能力
无需改动；可在同类业务模块的指引中把该组合作为推荐范式，并说明行级范围由业务 service 执行的边界。

问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 首次浏览器验收 verify-2 的 C01=failed 发生在应用尚未呈现 HR 页面/路由的阶段，不能据此判断框架模块失败；最终 verify-3 的 C01=passed。
  • UI 未由本评审人工查看：当前模型无法解析 PNG，ui.status=not-reviewed。仅存在截图文件不代表已做视觉评审。
  • 全过程 48 个错误信号已逐一处置（其中多数为探索性命令非零退出或测试台摩擦，2 条构成 F2 的 finding/resolved）；未覆盖的路径不推断无问题。
  • 本评审只读冻结输入，不能核对最新上游；confirmed 仅表示冻结材料支持评审判断，status=open 的改进项不代表上游已修复。
  • 行级/字段级越权用例未逐条执行：授权安全边界由应用 service 负责，框架侧只验证了决策与组合语义。
  • 快照未包含 3 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。
评审基线与版本指纹

评审针对已封存候选补丁与当时的依赖；可能在原搭建内或之后单独补跑。与 PR 或 NocoBase3 当前最新提交不是同一概念。评审只读冻结材料，引用运行日志不代表评审者重新复现。此报告未执行最新上游源码复核。

E4 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:236–248

Repository 用法与「易错边界」清单详列 filter/values/select、writePolicy 等，但未说明 createdAt/updatedAt 不会自动生成。

Resolve the manager once — in a route factory, or as a constructor dependency of a service — not inside every handler. `db.repository()`, `db.query()`, `db.builder()`, `db.collections()` and `db.transaction()` take the connection name as their last argument and use the default connection when it is omitted, so single-connection code never names one.

`filter`, `values`, `select` and `sort` each accept a builder callback or the equivalent serializable JSON AST, and mean the same thing either way. They do not accept a shorthand string array such as `['id', 'name']` or `['-createdAt']`. Field names are Collection logical names, never column names. A write returns a result object — the record is `result.record`, alongside `result.version` and the created relation targets — rather than the record itself. `findMany` is awaited for an array or iterated asynchronously to consume a large result incrementally.

The methods are `findOne`, `findMany`, `count`, `exists`, `aggregate`, `groupBy`, `createOne`, `createMany`, `updateOne`, `updateMany`, `upsertOne`, `deleteOne` and `deleteMany`; `describeMutation` and `validateMutation` report capabilities and pre-validate input, and `withPolicy` returns a scoped Repository.

Boundaries that are easy to get wrong:

- `findOne` takes the first match; `updateOne` and `deleteOne` require exactly one; `upsertOne` requires a unique equality condition.
- `updateMany` and `deleteMany` refuse an empty scope. Pass `all: true` only when the task explicitly calls for every row — never to make an error go away.
- `createMany` and `updateMany` do not accept nested relation writes. `createOne` accepts `create` and `connect` on a relation, not the full set.
- `disconnect` detaches; `delete` removes the target record. A relation target update or delete stays inside the current parent record's scope.
- Repository does not apply the application's permissions. A route handler decides what the caller may see and change _before_ anything reaches `filter`, `values` or `select`. `writePolicy` defaults to `true` for an internal Repository and `false` for one reached from an API route, so a route-facing Repository needs an explicit allow list.

Migrations under `database/main/migrations/` are the application's schema history. Seeds under `database/main/seeds/` insert records the application requires to run. Migrations create structure. Seeds never do.

E6 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/core/authorization.js:93–124

can() 仅当 effect 为 permit 时返回 true；require() 对非 permit 抛 AuthorizationDeniedError（应用回 403 PERMISSION_DENIED）；authorize() 返回完整 decision。

            identity,
            authorize: authorize,
            can: async (input) => (await this.authorizeWithGrants(request(input), grants, constraints, false)).effect === 'permit',
            require: async (input) => {
                const decision = await authorize(input);
                if (decision.effect !== 'permit') {

    async authorizeWithGrants(request, grants, constraints, resolveConditions = true) {
        const handler = resourceTypeHandler(this.resourceTypes, request.resource.type);
        if (!handler) {
            return deny('UNKNOWN_RESOURCE_TYPE', `No resource type "${request.resource.type}" is registered`);
        }
        const stored = request;
        try {
            const unrestricted = (await grants.unrestricted?.({
                principal: request.principal,
                ...(request.subjects === undefined
                    ? {}
                    : { subjects: request.subjects }),
            })) === true;
            const decision = unrestricted
                ? await handler.authorizeUnrestricted(stored)
                : await handler.authorize(stored, { grants, constraints });
            if (decision.effect === 'conditional' &&
                decision.conditions === undefined) {
                return deny('INVALID_CONDITIONAL_DECISION', `Resource type "${handler.type}" returned a conditional decision without conditions`);
            }

/**
 * A composite action is permitted by any valid grant on it; its branches are
 * checked separately. A stored grant that no longer expands permits nothing.
 */
async function authorizeComposite(registry, request, context) {
    const grants = await context.grants.resolve({
        principal: request.principal,
        ...(request.subjects === undefined ? {} : { subjects: request.subjects }),
        resource: request.resource,
        action: request.action,
    });
    const invalid = new Map();
    for (const grant of grants) {
        const reason = registry.validateGrant(grant);
        if (reason !== undefined)
            invalid.set(grant, reason);
    }
    const valid = grants.filter((grant) => !invalid.has(grant));
    if (valid.length)
        return {
            effect: 'permit',
            reasons: valid.map((grant) => ({
                code: 'GRANT_MATCHED',
                message: `${grant.source.plugin}:${grant.source.id} allows ${request.resource.id}.${request.action}`,
                details: { source: grant.source, policy: grant.policy },
            })),
        };
    if (invalid.size)

E9 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/api.js:14–34

app-plugin-authorization 的 DatabaseAuthorizationService 提供 authorizeRepository/policyFor/composeDatabasePolicies，用于把 Repository 绑定到复合动作。

    authorizeRepository(options) {
        const host = hosts.get(this);
        if (!host)
            throw new Error('The database plugin is not installed in an Authorization');
        return createCompositeRepositoryAuthorization(host, options);
    }
    async policyFor(collection, context, operation) {
        const resource = { type: 'database.collection', id: collection };
        const decide = async (action) => foldDecision(action, await context.authorize({
            resource,
            action,
            params: operation ? { operation } : {},
        }));
        const [read, create, update, remove] = await Promise.all([
            decide('read'),
            decide('create'),
            decide('update'),
            decide('delete'),
        ]);
        return { read, create, update, delete: remove };
    }

E10 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:68–71

SKILL 说明 composite/database.collection 是目录类型，复合动作只组合自己声明的 grant；简单 CRUD 用 authorizeRepository，多 scope/流程用自定义 handler。

- `settings`, `composite` and `database.collection` are catalog types: a check on an unregistered item or action is denied with `RESOURCE_ACTION_NOT_SUPPORTED`. `page`, `user`, `notification` and Hub types are record types that declare actions only; their grants use `id: '*'` for every record.
- A composite action composes exactly the grants it lists, never another composite. A data scope targets the one collection its grant actions name, whose type must declare `recordAccess` (`database.collection` does). Grant page access separately; a page grant never authorizes a server endpoint.
- `can` is for feature visibility. `require` needs an unconditional permit and rejects conditional decisions. Business endpoints use `authorize`, reject denial or missing policies, and consume `conditions.database` without resolving collection grants again.
- For simple business CRUD, keep the Repository route definition and bind each method to its composite action with `authz.database.authorizeRepository`. Multi-scope actions and workflow transitions need custom handlers. `authz.database.policyFor` folds collection grants; without its third argument it does not respect a business-action boundary.

E11 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/core-api.md:9–16

core-api 表格明确 can() 只对 permit 返回 true、require() 对非 permit 抛 403、conditional 决策必须交给理解其 conditions 的适配器执行。

| `AuthorizationContext` member              | Result and use                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `authorize({ resource, action, params? })` | The full decision: `permit`, `deny`, or `conditional` with `conditions`, and `reasons`                                                                                                                                   |
| `can(request)`                             | `true` only for `permit`; a business check reports feature availability, not record access                                                                                                                               |
| `require(request)`                         | Throws `AuthorizationDeniedError`, which the application answers as `403 PERMISSION_DENIED` with reason `AUTHORIZATION_DENIED` without an `onError`, unless the decision is `permit`; use for unconditional capabilities |
| `snapshot()`                               | `{ unrestricted, permissions }` for the client; not an executable data policy                                                                                                                                            |

Create one context per request and reuse it within that request only, never across identities. Its grant and rule reads are shared by the underlying checks of a business action. Execute a conditional decision only through the adapter that understands its conditions. Unknown types, unregistered catalog items or actions, conditional decisions without conditions and handler failures deny.

E12 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/runtime-api.md:135–137

runtime-api 在复合决策段末句写明「require rejects conditional decisions; can does not enforce rows」——这是指引中唯一直接点出该组合的句子。

```

A composite decision's `conditions` are `{ type: 'composite', checks, database }`: one `RepositoryPolicy` per composed collection, built from that action's grants only, and `checks` with the underlying decisions. A denied collection check yields `false` for that collection rather than denying the whole feature, and a Repository refuses to run under it; a granted check whose data scope selects nothing, such as a user in no department, yields a scope that matches no rows, so reads return an empty result without a special case. For `submit`, use both `conditions.database.quotes` and `.projects` inside the same transaction, check the quote's actual parent project through the project policy and include the expected state in the update predicate. Do not replace these policies with `authz.database.policyFor` without an operation: another action's grants could then widen access. `require` rejects conditional decisions; `can` does not enforce rows.

        });

E14 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:142–156

    async disable(userId) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.updateUser(userId, {
            disabledAt: new Date(),
        });
        await this.revokeSessions(userId);
        return (await this.get(userId));
    }
    async enable(userId) {
        await this.requireUser(userId);
        const context = await this.options.auth.administrationContext();
        await context.internalAdapter.updateUser(userId, { disabledAt: null });
        return (await this.get(userId));
    }

E15 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:138–140

初始管理员段声明「Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots」，但该约束只出现在 initialAdmin 段落。

## Initial administrator

Before the first seed run, set `users.initialAdmin.username`, `users.initialAdmin.email` and `users.initialAdmin.password` in application configuration to customize the bootstrap account. Omitting the entire node preserves `nocobase` / `admin@nocobase.com` / `admin123`; an explicit node requires a nonempty password and defaults the username to `nocobase` and the email to `admin@nocobase.com`. Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots, emails must be valid addresses, and both are stored lowercase. Initialization only runs when the user table is empty, hashes the password and grants the newly created account root permission through the authorization plugin. After initialization, configuration changes do not reset accounts or rerun the recorded seeds. The root permission seed selects the administrator by the same configured username. Use the user administration service for subsequent account changes. Do not edit historical seeds or add a competing administrator seed.

| Method                                              | Effect                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `list({ page, pageSize, search, status, userIds })` | Paged users; `pageSize` caps at 100; `status` is `'enabled'` or `'disabled'`                           |
| `get(userId)`                                       | One user or `undefined`                                                                                |
| `create({ name, email, username?, password })`      | Creates the user and a credential account; email and username are normalized to lower case             |
| `update(userId, { name?, email?, username? })`      | `username: null` clears it                                                                             |
| `disable(userId)`                                   | Sets `disabledAt`, deletes every session, disconnects realtime; sign-in returns `403 ACCOUNT_DISABLED` |
| `enable(userId)`                                    | Clears `disabledAt`; the user signs in again, old sessions stay gone                                   |
| `resetPassword(userId, password)`                   | Rehashes and revokes sessions                                                                          |
| `revokeSessions(userId)`                            | Deletes sessions and disconnects realtime without changing state                                       |
| `withConnection(connection)`                        | Binds every operation to a caller-owned transaction                                                    |

/**
 * The error categories an `/api` response may report, with the HTTP status each one maps to. The names and mapping are
 * Google's canonical error codes (AIP-193), so a client branches on a fixed, documented set rather than on whatever
 * each plugin invents. `reason` carries the plugin-specific detail.
 */
export const apiErrorStatusCodes = Object.freeze({
    INVALID_ARGUMENT: 400,
    FAILED_PRECONDITION: 400,
    OUT_OF_RANGE: 400,
    UNAUTHENTICATED: 401,
    PERMISSION_DENIED: 403,
    NOT_FOUND: 404,
    ALREADY_EXISTS: 409,
    ABORTED: 409,
    RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED: 429,
    INTERNAL: 500,
    UNIMPLEMENTED: 501,
    UNAVAILABLE: 503,
    DEADLINE_EXCEEDED: 504,
});

E18 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/routes.js:1–18

defineApiRoutes/defineRootRoutes/defineHttpMiddleware 冻结为 scope 明确的贡献对象，是应用注册路由的公开入口。

export function defineHttpMiddleware(options) {
    return Object.freeze({
        name: options.name,
        register: options.register,
    });
}
export function defineApiRoutes(createRouter) {
    return Object.freeze({
        scope: 'api',
        createRouter,
    });
}
export function defineRootRoutes(createRouter) {
    return Object.freeze({
        scope: 'root',
        createRouter,
    });
}

E19 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:5–14

server-routes 明确 /api 路由路径不再重复 /api、不写部署前缀，且每个 /api 路由必须 describeRoute() 且用 apiValidator() 校验，「without a declaration is a defect」。

## Two kinds

| Function             | Path in code     | Final URL        | For                                              |
| -------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `defineApiRoutes()`  | `/orders`        | `/api/orders`    | Application APIs the browser calls               |
| `defineRootRoutes()` | `/callbacks/pay` | `/callbacks/pay` | Webhooks, OAuth callbacks, protocol entry points |

Do not repeat `/api` in the path, and never write the deployment base path such as `/main` — both are added by the runtime.

Name paths, shape responses and errors, validate input and declare each route for the API document as [HTTP API design](http-api.md) describes. Every `/api` route carries a `describeRoute()` and validates its input with `apiValidator()`, both from `@nocobase/app-server/router`; a route without a declaration is a defect.

E21 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/dist/server/store.js:71–128

in-app 插件提供 DatabaseInAppStore：按 userId 持久化投递、unreadOnly 列表与 countUnread，说明站内信是数据库支撑的持久 inbox。

export class DatabaseInAppStore {
    database;
    constructor(database) {
        this.database = database;
    }
    async deliver(input) {
        const item = {
            id: randomUUID(),
            deliveryId: input.deliveryId,
            notificationId: input.notificationId,
            userId: input.userId,
            title: input.message.title,
            body: input.message.body,
            target: validateNotificationTarget(input.message.target),
            createdAt: input.createdAt,
            updatedAt: input.createdAt,
        };
        try {
            await this.database
                .query()
                .insertInto('notificationInAppItems')
                .values(toRow(item))
                .execute();
        }
        catch (error) {
            const existing = await this.database
                .query()
                .selectFrom('notificationInAppItems')
                .selectAll()
                .where('deliveryId', '=', input.deliveryId)
                .executeTakeFirst();
            if (existing)
                return fromRow(existing);
            throw error;
        }
        return item;
    }
    async list(input) {
        let query = this.database
            .query()
            .selectFrom('notificationInAppItems')
            .selectAll()
            .where('userId', '=', input.userId)
            .orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')
            .orderBy('id', 'desc')
            .limit(input.limit ?? 25);
        if (input.unreadOnly)
            query = query.where('readAt', 'is', null);
        if (input.before)
            query = query.where((builder) => builder.or([
                builder('createdAt', '<', input.before?.createdAt),
                builder.and([
                    builder('createdAt', '=', input.before?.createdAt),
                    builder('id', '<', input.before?.id),
                ]),
            ]));
        return (await query.execute()).map(fromRow);
    }

E22 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification/SKILL.md:19–24

notification SKILL 指引从 concepts/integration/sending/diagnostics/extensions 到验证的完整路径，并强调保留 Notification/Delivery/Attempt 历史。

1. Read [Notification concepts](references/notification-concepts.md) for package ownership and delivery status semantics.
2. For plugin registration, configuration, migrations, or test APIs, read [Integration and configuration](references/integration-and-configuration.md). Configure a Channel name mapped to one flat Provider configuration.
3. For sending or typed consumers, read [Sending notifications](references/sending-notifications.md). Resolve `notificationServiceToken`; construct complete messages keyed by Channel name and use native recipient addresses. Validate the intended external effect before sending.
4. For failures and retries, read [Delivery diagnostics](references/delivery-diagnostics.md). Preserve Notification, Delivery, Attempt, and retry-audit history; distinguish accepted submission from final delivery.
5. For custom transports, read [Channel and Provider extensions](references/channel-and-provider-extensions.md). Register globally unique Provider definitions through `notificationExtensionRegistryToken` and retain the queue/lease/retry lifecycle.
6. Verify changed packages and consumers with lint, typecheck, tests, and build. Cover atomic validation, independent delivery, recipient rules, and retries bound to original identities.

E23 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification-in-app/references/inbox-integration.md:3–10

inbox-integration 列出必需包与注册顺序：核心 notification 插件应在 in-app 插件之前注册，in-app 提供 inbox 持久化与 HTTP 路由。

## Required packages

- `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication` supplies the authenticated user and session.
- `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/server` supplies inbox persistence and HTTP routes.
- `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/server` is required when the application needs the registered `in-app` Channel and Provider contribution.
- `@nocobase/app-client` supplies the application-scoped HTTP and realtime clients used by the Client inbox page.

Register the core notification Server plugin before the in-app Server plugin. The in-app routes can operate without the core plugin, but notification delivery through the `in-app` Channel cannot.

E24 · app/server/hr/service.ts:86–106

应用注释说明托管集合 NOT NULL createdAt/updatedAt 无数据库默认，因此 createStamp/updateStamp/createdStamp 在每次写入自带时间戳——这是对框架无自动时间戳的绕行。

/**
 * The managed collections declare NOT NULL `createdAt`/`updatedAt` with no
 * database default, exactly as the framework's own tables do, so every write
 * supplies its own timestamps.
 */
function createStamp(): { createdAt: Date; updatedAt: Date } {
  const now = new Date();
  return { createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
}

function updateStamp(): { updatedAt: Date } {
  return { updatedAt: new Date() };
}

/**
 * The notification table is an append-only log with no `updatedAt` column, so
 * it stamps only the creation time.
 */
function createdStamp(): { createdAt: Date } {
  return { createdAt: new Date() };
}

E25 · app/server/routes/hr.ts:92–108

应用的路由门禁最终用 context.can({ resource: HR_RESOURCE, action })；注释解释 require 拒绝 conditional，不能门禁 composite。

  const require = async (
    context: AuthorizationContext,
    action: HrAction,
  ): Promise<void> => {
    // A composite action composes database grants, whose own decisions are
    // `conditional` because they carry a row scope. `require` rejects anything
    // other than a plain `permit`, so it cannot gate a composite; `can` is the
    // action-level check. Row scoping and field confidentiality are enforced by
    // the service, which is where a data scope that resolves to nothing turns
    // into an empty result rather than a refusal.
    if (await context.can({ resource: HR_RESOURCE, action })) return;
    throw new HrError(
      'PERMISSION_DENIED',
      'AUTHORIZATION_DENIED',
      `Missing permission: ${HR_RESOURCE.id}.${action}`,
    );
  };

E29 · app/server/hr/service.ts:1030–1058

应用自建 listNotifications 与 markNotificationRead：按 userId 过滤、校验归属后置 read=true，未走框架通知插件的 inbox API。

  async listNotifications(actor: HrActor): Promise<HrNotificationRow[]> {
    this.assertMember(actor);
    return this.notifications().findMany({
      filter: { userId: actor.userId },
      sort: (sort) => sort.field('id').desc(),
      limit: 50,
    });
  }

  async markNotificationRead(
    actor: HrActor,
    id: number,
  ): Promise<HrNotificationRow> {
    this.assertMember(actor);
    const existing = await this.notifications().findOne({ filter: { id } });
    if (!existing || existing.userId !== actor.userId) {
      throw new HrError(
        'NOT_FOUND',
        'HR_NOTIFICATION_NOT_FOUND',
        'No such notification.',
      );
    }
    return (
      await this.notifications().updateOne({
        filter: { id },
        values: { read: true },
      })
    ).record;
  }

Configuration\n\nConnections are declared in code and overridden per environment:\n\n| Location                    | Responsibility                                                        |\n| --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `server/config/database.ts` | Connection defaults through `defineAppDatabaseConfig`                 |\n| `config.yml`                | Environment overrides including credentials — gitignored              |\n| `config.example.yml`        | The documented structure and credential placeholders; keep it in step |\n\n```ts\nimport { defineAppDatabaseConfig } from '@nocobase/app-server/database';\n\nexport default defineAppDatabaseConfig(({ paths }) => ({\n  default: 'main',\n  connections: {\n    main: {\n      dialect: 'sqlite',\n      filename: paths.storage('database.sqlite'),\n      schemaManagement: 'managed',\n      debug: false,\n    },\n  },\n}));\n```\n\nThe dialects are `sqlite`, `postgres`, `mysql`, `mssql`, `oracle`, `dameng`, `kingbase` and `oceanbase`. Each is an optional peer of `@nocobase/app-server` shipped as its own package: install `@nocobase/db-<dialect>` into `dependencies` — so it reaches the deployed server — and configure the dialect. No import or driver registration is needed, and the dialect package brings its own underlying driver.\n\nSwitching a dialect means changing **both** layers. `config.yml` deep-merges into the code defaults, so editing only the code leaves a stale YAML override in place — with fields belonging to the previous dialect still applied. Remove `filename` from a connection switched away from SQLite. In YAML, `database:` is an alias for `filename` and takes precedence over it, which is the usual reason a changed `filename` appears to do nothing.\n\n`schemaManagement` decides who owns the schema. A connection the application owns is `'managed'` and has migrations; a database owned by another system is `'external'`, which rejects builder DDL and migrations while still allowing record reads and writes, subject to the database account's own permissions. `pnpm nocobase db apply --all` reports external connections as skipped, and targeting one explicitly is an error.\n\nPassing `pnpm typecheck` validates neither the YAML contents nor connectivity. Only starting the application against the target proves either.\n\nThe mechanics belong to the application rather than to this package: how `pnpm nocobase config init` configures one, the per-dialect connection fields and defaults, driver installation and native binaries, and the typing of `defineAppDatabaseConfig` are in `.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/database-connections.md`.\n\nKeep `@nocobase/db` itself in `dependencies`, not `devDependencies`: the deployed server resolves it at runtime, and TypeScript reads that declaration to infer the database configuration.\n\n## 2. E57 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:70–70

该 SKILL 的 Enforcement rules 明写：`can` 用于功能可见性；`require` 需要 unconditional permit 并拒绝 conditional decisions；业务端点改用 `authorize`。这是指引中直接陈述 require/conditional 语义的显著章节（Implement 步骤 4 未含该句）。

- `can` is for feature visibility. `require` needs an unconditional permit and rejects conditional decisions. Business endpoints use `authorize`, reject denial or missing policies, and consume `conditions.database` without resolving collection grants again.

原文件 SHA-256：0ae2663fd2f0a5522fd2dd952eea86cf51f1f89024423e5c3efa207a436b7e69

E58 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/core-api.md:13–13

core-api 的 `require(request)` 行说明：除非决策为 `permit` 否则抛 `AuthorizationDeniedError`（403 PERMISSION_DENIED），并注明 use for unconditional capabilities；但未点名 composite 决策恒为 conditional。

| `require(request)`                         | Throws `AuthorizationDeniedError`, which the application answers as `403 PERMISSION_DENIED` with reason `AUTHORIZATION_DENIED` without an `onError`, unless the decision is `permit`; use for unconditional capabilities |

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

员工档案与入离职

部门与员工可新增修改，编辑时原值完整回填；新建员工勾选开通账号后自动生成登录用户；离职后账号禁用、重新登录被拒，原请假记录保留。

请假审批闭环

员工查看本人资料并自助申请请假；主管仅见本部门申请，空原因驳回被拦截；员工收到含原因的驳回通知后可修改重提，主管批准后员工收到批准通知。

资料权限隔离

身份证号与合同号仅本人和人事可见，主管仅见姓名、岗位与请假记录，员工直接访问员工与审批页面被拒绝。

首页指标

首页展示在职人数、待入职人员和待处理申请，并随入职、离职与审批结果动态变化。

01管理员登录 HR 系统
→
02维护部门与员工档案
→
03入职勾选开通账号
→
04员工申请请假
→
05主管驳回并说明原因
→
06员工修改后重新提交
→
07主管批准并通知员工
→
08离职停用账号

业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

Acceptance record

验收记录

1 项要求
报告通过 1报告未通过 0未验证 0

每项直接显示结果；展开可看原始要求、操作记录、观察结果和截图。证据批注单独显示，不改写原始验收状态。

01

我是人事负责人，想给一家80人的公司搭建轻量HR系统，管理部门、员工档案、入职和离职。入职时开通账号，离职时停用账号，停用后不能继续访问，原有业务记录保留。 员工可以查看自己的资料并申请请假，主管处理本部门申请；驳回必须说明原因，员工可以修改后重新提交，处理结果通知申请人。身份证明、劳动合同等资料及附件仅本人和人事可查看，主管只能查看部门员工的姓名、岗位和请假记录。首页展示在职人数、待入职人员和待处理申请。

应用未提供文件附件上传控件(员工/请假表单无 file 输入)，敏感资料以身份证号/合同号字段实现并按角色限权。

A1 · 15 条操作 · 12 条观察 · 21 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

我是人事负责人，想给一家80人的公司搭建轻量HR系统，管理部门、员工档案、入职和离职。入职时开通账号，离职时停用账号，停用后不能继续访问，原有业务记录保留。 员工可以查看自己的资料并申请请假，主管处理本部门申请；驳回必须说明原因，员工可以修改后重新提交，处理结果通知申请人。身份证明、劳动合同等资料及附件仅本人和人事可查看，主管只能查看部门员工的姓名、岗位和请假记录。首页展示在职人数、待入职人员和待处理申请。

实际操作

  1. 以管理员 nocobase 登录，确认已进入 HR 业务界面(首页/部门/员工/我的请假/请假审批/我的资料)。
  2. 首页核对指标: 在职人数、待入职人员、待处理请假申请均有数值展示。
  3. 部门管理: 新建部门 测试部(代码 TEST)，随后编辑原值回填正确(测试部/TEST/说明)并修改经理与说明后保存成功。
  4. 员工档案: 查看 张明 详情，可见工号/部门/状态/邮箱/电话/身份证号/合同号;点击 Edit 原值全部回填，修改 Notes 后保存并复核成功。
  5. 入职开通账号: 新建员工 测试员工647(E9901,研发部)并勾选 Open an account;保存成功后在 User management 中确认自动生成账号 测试员工647 @e9901。
  6. 员工自助: 以 e9901 登录，My profile 显示本人身份证号/合同号资料;提交年假申请 2026-10-12~10-14，My leave 显示 Pending。
  7. 主管审批: 以研发部主管 韩磊(manager)登录，Leave approvals 仅显示本部门申请;对 e9901 的申请先空原因点 Reject 被拦截(提示 A rejection must state a reason)，填写驳回原因后成功驳回。
  8. 驳回通知与重提: e9901 登录后在通知中收到 'Leave request rejected' 并含驳回原因;My leave 中该申请显示 Rejected+原因，点击 Edit and resubmit 原值回填，改期与原因后重新提交为 Pending。
  9. 主管批准: 韩磊 再次审批，选择 Approve 成功;Approved 过滤中确认 e9901 申请为 Approved。
  10. 结果通知: e9901 登录后通知中出现 'Leave request approved'。
  11. 权限隔离: 主管 韩磊 的员工详情仅含姓名/岗位/工号/部门/状态，无身份证号/合同号/邮箱/电话;员工直接访问 hr-employees 与 hr-approvals 均返回 Access denied。
  12. 人事可见: 以 hrlead(冯雪)登录，员工详情可见身份证号/合同号，且请假审批可查看全公司申请。
  13. 离职停用: 以 hrlead 对 测试员工647 执行 Offboard，状态变为 Offboarded、账号在用户管理中变为 Disabled;用 e9901 重新登录返回 Forbidden 无法进入。
  14. 记录保留: 离职后 测试员工647 的已批准请假记录仍出现在审批列表中，员工档案仍保留;首页在职人数由 11 降为 10。
  15. 控制台/网络: 未发现 5xx;仅出现一次性预期 403(被停用账号登录)。

观察结果

  1. 首页显示 Active employees 10、Pending onboarding 2、Pending leave requests 2，离职后在职人数降为 10。
  2. 部门 测试部(TEST)新建成功，编辑弹窗回填原值后可修改经理为许静、说明并保存生效。
  3. 员工 张明 详情显示身份证号 110101199001011001 与合同号 HT-E1001;Edit 表单原值回填(姓名/工号/邮箱/电话/部门/岗位/状态/日期/证件/合同/备注)。
  4. 新建员工勾选开通账号后，用户管理出现 测试员工647 @e9901 · qaemp647@example.com;员工登录后收到 'Your account is ready' 通知。
  5. 员工本人 My profile 可见本人身份证号/合同号;提交年假后 My leave 显示 Pending。
  6. 主管韩磊仅看到本部门(研发部)员工与请假申请，看不到销售部王强的申请;员工详情不含证件/合同/邮箱/电话。
  7. 空原因点击 Reject 未提交且提示必须填写原因;填写原因后驳回成功。
  8. 员工收到含驳回原因的拒绝通知，可在 My leave 中回填并修改后重新提交为 Pending，随后主管批准。
  9. 员工收到 'Leave request approved' 通知;员工访问 hr-employees/hr-approvals 均显示 Access denied。
  10. 人事 hrlead 可查看身份证号/合同号及全公司请假记录。
  11. 离职后账号状态 Disabled、员工状态 Offboarded、登录返回 Forbidden;离职员工已批准请假记录保留。
  12. 应用未提供文件附件上传控件(员工/请假表单无 file 输入)，敏感资料以身份证号/合同号字段实现并按角色限权。

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed
